Agenda Title: Discuss and Possible Approval of Resolution 2026-08-577 Approving the
Purchase of Fire Rescue Dispatch Equipment (City Manager)


Requested Action: Approval

I. Report in Brief:

The purchase of the Dispatch equipment is to proceed with the new county cad system. This will
replace the outdated toning system in the station, while putting computers in all the response
vehicles to get updated information and GIS tracking.


Background:

Two to three years ago, the county began installing a new computer and CAD system. This
system transitioned to a countywide dispatch system where all departments can communicate
with each other. This system is almost complete, and the equipment purchase is part of the new
system.

Recommendation:

Staff recommends the purchase of the dispatch equipment.

                                RESOLUTION NO. 2026-08-577

      A RESOLUTION OF THE CITY OF LYNN HAVEN, FLORIDA; APPROVING
      AGREEMENTS WITH DELL TECHNOLOGIES, HOWARD TECHNOLOGY
      SOLUTIONS, AND MOBILE COMMUNICATIONS AMERICA, FOR THE
      PURCHASE OF A FIRE DISPATCH EQUIPMENT, IN THE TOTAL
      AMOUNT OF $43,557.42.

BE IT RESOLVED by the City Commission of the City of Lynn Haven that:
   1. The appropriate officers of the City are authorized to accept and deliver on behalf of the City
      that certain proposal from Dell Technologies, dated July 30, 2026, for the purchase of 10 Dell
      Pro Rugged 14 Laptops and adapters, in the total amount of Twenty Three Thousand, Six
      Hundred Seventy Dollars and 10/100 ($23,670.10), in the form attached as Exhibit A-1 and
      presented to the Commission today. By accepting this proposal, the City accepts the same
      terms and conditions as were received by the Florida Department of Management Services
      through an advertised competitive bidding process, Contract C000001116132, in the form
      attached as Exhibit A-2.
   2. The appropriate officers of the City are authorized to accept and deliver on behalf of the City
      that certain proposal from Howard Technology Solutions, dated July 14, 2026, for the purchase
      of accessories, in the total amount of Four Thousand, Six Hundred Sixty Two Dollars and
      00/100 ($4,662,00), in the form attached as Exhibit B-1 and presented to the Commission
      today.
   3. The appropriate officers of the City are authorized to accept and deliver on behalf of the City
      that certain proposal from Mobile Communications America, dated June 29, 2026, for the
      purchase of an analog board, alerting controller, antenna and mount, in the total amount of
      Fifteen Thousand, Two Hundred Twenty Five Dollars and 32/100 ($15,225.32), in the form
      attached as Exhibit C-1 and presented to the Commission today. By accepting this proposal,
      the City accepts the same terms and conditions as were received by Sourcewell through an
      advertised competitive bidding process, Contract 020625-MOT, in the form attached as Exhibit
      C-2.

      THIS RESOLUTION shall be effective immediately upon passage.

     PASSED AND ADOPTED this _____ day of ______________, 2026, by the LYNN
 HAVEN CITY COMMISSION meeting in regular session.

Agenda Title: Discuss & Possible Approval on directing staff to hold a special meeting to
approve ballot language for the November referendum on charter changes. (City Manager)


Requested Action:

I. Report in Brief:

The Charter Review Committee has been working on updates to the City Charter.


Background:

The next round of ballot language could potentially be approved for the November Ballot. A
special meeting will likely need to be held to make the August 20th deadline as given by the
Supervisor of Elections. Ms. Ward's email is below:

We need the approved language no later than August 20th. There are already 3 State
Constitutional Amendments, so we would have space for up to 3 additional questions for
the City. Anything more than that could push us to a two-sheet ballot which would get
complicated if it is only for the City of Lynn Haven and not ideal for voters or our
equipment. Let me know if you would like to discuss further. No cost as long as it fits on
our regular ballot.
